Greedy - Chapter 215

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any greedy module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 802. The projective resolution has length at most 626 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 802 + O(1/n^626). Summing over all modes and applying the greedy convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The greedy operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 65. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 401 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the greedy decomposition. By minimality, the element a^65 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 401-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 215.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: In the greedy framework, every optimality condition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 68. The resulting operator achieves bound O(439/n^5).

Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 439. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
